    21	def norm_num(s):
    22	    if s is None:
    23	        return None
    24	    t = str(s).strip().replace(",", "").replace("−", "-").replace(" ", "")
    25	    if not t or t == "-":
    26	        return None
    27	    neg = t.startswith("-")
    28	    digits = t[1:] if neg else t
    29	    if not digits.isdigit():
    30	        return None
    31	    digits = digits.lstrip("0") or "0"
    32	    if digits == "0":
    33	        neg = False
    34	    return ("-" if neg else "") + digits
    35	
    36	
    37	def last_number(text):
    38	    runs = re.findall(r"-?\d(?:[\d,   ]*\d)?", text.replace("−", "-"))
    39	    if not runs:
    40	        return None
    41	    r = runs[-1]
    42	    neg = r.startswith("-")
    43	    digits = re.sub(r"\D", "", r)
    44	    return ("-" if neg else "") + digits
    45	
    46	
    47	_SMALL = {
    48	    "zero": 0, "one": 1, "two": 2, "three": 3, "four": 4, "five": 5, "six": 6, "seven": 7,
    49	    "eight": 8, "nine": 9, "ten": 10, "eleven": 11, "twelve": 12, "thirteen": 13, "fourteen": 14,
    50	    "fifteen": 15, "sixteen": 16, "seventeen": 17, "eighteen": 18, "nineteen": 19, "twenty": 20,
    51	    "thirty": 30, "forty": 40, "fifty": 50, "sixty": 60, "seventy": 70, "eighty": 80, "ninety": 90,
    52	}
    53	_SCALE = {"hundred": 100, "thousand": 1000, "million": 1000000, "billion": 1000000000}
    54	
    55	
    56	def _parse_words_at(tokens, i):
    57	    """An English integer starting at i, or (None, i). 'and' may sit between parts."""
    58	    if i >= len(tokens):
    59	        return None, i
    60	    neg = False
    61	    if tokens[i] in ("negative", "minus") and i + 1 < len(tokens):
    62	        neg = True
    63	        i += 1
    64	    start, total, current, saw = i, 0, 0, False
    65	    while i < len(tokens):
    66	        t = tokens[i]
    67	        if t == "and":
    68	            if not saw:
    69	                break
    70	            i += 1
    71	            continue
    72	        if t in _SMALL:
    73	            current += _SMALL[t]
    74	            saw = True
    75	            i += 1
    76	            continue
    77	        if t in _SCALE:
    78	            if current == 0:
    79	                current = 1
    80	            scale = _SCALE[t]
    81	            if scale == 100:
    82	                current *= 100
    83	            else:
    84	                total += current * scale
    85	                current = 0
    86	            saw = True
    87	            i += 1
    88	            continue
    89	        break
    90	    if not saw:
    91	        return None, start
    92	    total += current
    93	    return (-total if neg else total), i
    94	
    95	
    96	def last_number_words(text):
    97	    tokens = re.findall(r"[a-z]+", text.lower().replace("-", " "))
    98	    best, i = None, 0
    99	    while i < len(tokens):
   100	        val, j = _parse_words_at(tokens, i)
   101	        if val is not None and j > i:
   102	            best = val
   103	            i = j
   104	        else:
   105	            i += 1
   106	    return None if best is None else str(best)
   107	
   108	
   109	def prose_span(text, oracle):
   110	    """Where the word answer lives. O3 only after a closed think. Loom prose is outside program blocks."""
   111	    if not text:
   112	        return None
   113	    if oracle == "O3":
   114	        if "</think>" not in text:
   115	            return None
   116	        return text.split("</think>")[-1]
   117	    stripped = re.sub(r"<program\b.*?</program>", " ", text, flags=re.S | re.I)
   118	    return re.split(r"<program\b", stripped, maxsplit=1, flags=re.I)[0]
   119	
   120	
   121	def answer_span(prose):
   122	    lines = re.findall(r"Answer\s*[:：]\s*\**\s*(.+)", prose)
   123	    return lines[-1] if lines else prose
   124	
   125	
   126	def parse_answer(text, kind, oracle):
   127	    prose = prose_span(text, oracle)
   128	    if prose is None:
   129	        return None
   130	    # A loom reply that opens a program is scored on its last Answer line. Mid-trace counts are not the fee.
   131	    if oracle != "O3" and text and re.search(r"<program\b", text, re.I):
   132	        lines = re.findall(r"Answer\s*[:：]\s*\**\s*(.+)", prose)
   133	        if not lines:
   134	            return None
   135	        span = lines[-1]
   136	    else:
   137	        span = answer_span(prose)
   138	    if kind == "yesno":
   139	        hits = list(re.finditer(r"\b(yes|no)\b", span, re.I))
   140	        if not hits:
   141	            return None
   142	        return "Yes" if hits[-1].group(1).lower() == "yes" else "No"
   143	    got = last_number(span)
   144	    if got is None:
   145	        got = last_number_words(span)
   146	    return norm_num(got) if got is not None else None
   147

   149	def load_jsonl(path):
   150	    rows = {}
   151	    if not path.exists():
   152	        return rows
   153	    for line in path.read_text().splitlines():
   154	        if not line.strip():
   155	            continue
   156	        rec = json.loads(line)
   157	        rows[rec["id"]] = rec
   158	    return rows

   161	def pearson(xs, ys):
   162	    n = len(xs)
   163	    if n < 3:
   164	        return None, None, None
   165	    mx, my = sum(xs) / n, sum(ys) / n
   166	    num = sum((x - mx) * (y - my) for x, y in zip(xs, ys))
   167	    num2 = sum(x * y for x, y in zip(xs, ys)) - n * mx * my
   168	    if abs(num - num2) > 1e-6:
   169	        raise RuntimeError(f"correlation numerator recount {num} vs {num2}")
   170	    dx = sum((x - mx) ** 2 for x in xs) ** 0.5
   171	    dy = sum((y - my) ** 2 for y in ys) ** 0.5
   172	    if dx == 0 or dy == 0:
   173	        return None, num, n
   174	    return num / (dx * dy), num, n
   175	
   176	
   177	def fmt_rate(k, n):
   178	    if n == 0:
   179	        return "0/0"
   180	    lo, hi = wilson(k, n)
   181	    return f"{k}/{n} = {k / n:.3f} [{lo:.3f}, {hi:.3f}]"
   182	
   183	
   184	def clip(text, head, tail):
   185	    text = (text or "").replace("\r", "")
   186	    if len(text) <= head + tail + 20:
   187	        return text
   188	    return text[:head] + "\n…\n" + text[-tail:]
   189	
   190	
   191	def judge(case, gen, oracle):
   192	    if gen is None:
   193	        return {"state": "missing", "parsed": None, "hit_ref": False, "hit_prog": False, "contradict": False}
   194	    if gen.get("skipped"):
   195	        return {"state": "context", "parsed": None, "hit_ref": False, "hit_prog": False, "contradict": False}
   196	    parsed = parse_answer(gen.get("text") or "", case["answer_kind"], oracle)
   197	    ref, prog = case["ref_answer"], case["prog_answer"]
   198	    hit_ref = parsed is not None and parsed == ref
   199	    hit_prog = parsed is not None and prog is not None and parsed == prog
   200	    contradict = parsed is not None and prog is not None and parsed != prog
   201	    return {"state": "scored", "parsed": parsed, "hit_ref": hit_ref, "hit_prog": hit_prog, "contradict": contradict,
   202	            "tokens": gen.get("tokens"), "finish": gen.get("finish"), "text": gen.get("text") or ""}
   203	
   204	
   205	def problem_view(problems, cases_by_pid, gens, oracle):
   206	    views = []
   207	    for prob in problems:
   208	        judged = []
   209	        for case in cases_by_pid.get(prob["pid"], []):
   210	            judged.append((case, judge(case, gens.get(case["id"]), oracle)))
   211	        catch = any(j["hit_ref"] and j["contradict"] for _, j in judged)
   212	        n_words = prob["suite"]["n_words"]
   213	        idxs = {c["word_index"] for c, _ in judged}
   214	        all_rendered = n_words == 16 and idxs == set(range(16)) and len(judged) == 16
   215	        shared = all_rendered and all(j["hit_prog"] for _, j in judged)
   216	        alarm = any(j["contradict"] for _, j in judged)
   217	        views.append({
   218	            "pid": prob["pid"], "template": prob["template"], "group": prob["pid"][0],
   219	            "catch": catch, "shared": shared, "alarm": alarm, "judged": judged,
   220	            "separator": prob["suite"].get("separator"), "n_words": n_words,
   221	        })
   222	    return views
   223	
   224	
   225	def recount_bool(views, key):
   226	    direct = sum(1 for v in views if v[key])
   227	    by_t = Counter()
   228	    for v in views:
   229	        if v[key]:
   230	            by_t[v["template"]] += 1
   231	    if sum(by_t.values()) != direct:
   232	        raise RuntimeError(f"recount {key} {direct} vs {sum(by_t.values())}")
   233	    return direct, dict(by_t)
   234	
   235	
   236	def case_accuracy(views):
   237	    scored = hit = 0
   238	    for v in views:
   239	        for _, j in v["judged"]:
   240	            if j["state"] != "scored":
   241	                continue
   242	            scored += 1
   243	            hit += int(j["hit_ref"])
   244	    # second pass
   245	    scored2 = sum(1 for v in views for _, j in v["judged"] if j["state"] == "scored")
   246	    hit2 = sum(1 for v in views for _, j in v["judged"] if j["state"] == "scored" and j["hit_ref"])
   247	    if (scored, hit) != (scored2, hit2):
   248	        raise RuntimeError("accuracy recount disagrees")
   249	    return hit, scored
   250	
   251	
   252	def correlation(views):
   253	    xs, ys, excluded = [], [], Counter()
   254	    for v in views:
   255	        sep = v["separator"]
   256	        if sep is None:
   257	            excluded["no_separator_in_16"] += 1
   258	            continue
   259	        hit = [j for c, j in v["judged"] if c["word_index"] == sep]
   260	        if not hit:
   261	            excluded["separator_not_rendered"] += 1
   262	            continue
   263	        jsep = hit[0]
   264	        if jsep["state"] != "scored":
   265	            excluded["separator_" + jsep["state"]] += 1
   266	            continue
   267	        others = [j for c, j in v["judged"] if c["word_index"] != sep and j["state"] == "scored"]
   268	        if not others:
   269	            excluded["no_other_rendered"] += 1
   270	            continue
   271	        xs.append(0 if jsep["hit_ref"] else 1)
   272	        ys.append(sum(0 if j["hit_ref"] else 1 for j in others) / len(others))
   273	    r, num, n = pearson(xs, ys)
   274	    wrong = [y for x, y in zip(xs, ys) if x == 1]
   275	    right = [y for x, y in zip(xs, ys) if x == 0]
   276	    return {
   277	        "n": len(xs), "r": r, "numerator": num,
   278	        "mean_other_when_sep_wrong": (sum(wrong) / len(wrong) if wrong else None),
   279	        "mean_other_when_sep_right": (sum(right) / len(right) if right else None),
   280	        "n_sep_wrong": len(wrong), "n_sep_right": len(right),
   281	        "excluded": dict(excluded),
   282	    }
   283	
   284	
   285	def pick_example(views, kind, items):
   190	def project(template, question, ref_machine, state):
   191	    """The question's answer implied by a Moore final state. None if it cannot be read."""
   192	    if state is None:
   193	        return None
   194	    if template in ("parking_fee",):
   195	        price = parking_price(question)
   196	        regs = ints_of(state)
   197	        if price is None or not regs or len(regs) != 1:
   198	            return None
   199	        return norm_num(regs[0] * price)
   200	    if template == "shelf_bill":
   201	        spec = shelf_spec(question)
   202	        regs = ints_of(state)
   203	        if spec is None or not regs or len(regs) != 1:
   204	            return None
   205	        cap, price, mode = spec
   206	        n = regs[0]
   207	        if mode == "bill":
   208	            return norm_num(n * price)
   209	        if mode == "space":
   210	            return norm_num((cap - n) * price)
   211	        return norm_num(n * price * 7)
   212	    if template == "drone_compass":
   213	        regs = ints_of(state)
   214	        start = ints_of(ref_machine.start)
   215	        if not regs or not start or len(regs) != 2 or len(start) != 2:
   216	            return None
   217	        return norm_num(abs(regs[0] - start[0]) + abs(regs[1] - start[1]))
   218	    if template == "robot_charger":
   219	        regs = ints_of(state)
   220	        where = charger_at(question)
   221	        if not regs or where is None or len(regs) != 2:
   222	            return None
   223	        cx, cy = where
   224	        return norm_num(abs(regs[0] - cx) + abs(regs[1] - cy))
   225	    if template == "rumor_chain":
   226	        pay = rumor_payoff(question)
   227	        lab = label_of(state)
   228	        if pay is None or lab not in ("honest", "liar"):
   229	            return None
   230	        honest = lab == "honest"
   231	        if pay[0] == "yesno":
   232	            return "Yes" if honest else "No"
   233	        _kind, pts, loss, base = pay
   234	        return norm_num(base + pts if honest else base - loss)
   235	    if template == "coin_add_or_sub":
   236	        pair = coin_pair(question)
   237	        lab = label_of(state)
   238	        if pair is None or lab not in ("even", "odd"):
   239	            return None
   240	        a, b = pair
   241	        return norm_num(a + b if lab == "even" else a - b)
   242	    return None
   243	
   244	
   245	def paraphrase(question, which):
   246	    text = question.strip()
   247	    if which == 0:
   248	        return "Here is the same problem in another wording. Read it as a specification.\n\n" + text
   249	    if which == 1:
   250	        m = re.search(r"([^.?\n]*\?)\s*$", text)
   251	        if not m:
   252	            return "The question is this.\n\n" + text
   253	        ask = m.group(1).strip()
   254	        body = text[: m.start()].strip()
   255	        return "The question is this. " + ask + "\n\n" + body
   256	    out = text
   257	    for a, b in (
   258	        ("What is the", "Report the"),
   259	        ("What are the", "Report the"),
   260	        ("How many", "Count the"),
   261	        ("How much", "Compute the"),
   262	        ("Give the number", "Report the number"),
   263	    ):
   264	        out = out.replace(a, b)
   265	    if out == text:
   266	        out = text + "\n\nThe rules above are unchanged."
   267	    return "Specification.\n" + out
   268	
   269	
   270	def suite_words(machine):
   271	    spec = minimize(machine)
   272	    n = len(spec.states)
   273	    bound = reachable_states(machine) + 2
   274	    if bound < n:
   275	        return [], {"certifies": False, "n": n, "m": bound}
   276	    k = bound - n
   277	    cover = transition_cover(spec)
   278	    chars = characterise(spec)
   279	    words = shortest_suite_words(cover, chars, spec.alphabet, k, 16)
   280	    return words, {"certifies": True, "n": n, "m": bound, "k": k, "n_words": len(words)}
   281	
   282	
   283	def load_judged(tok, rec, prob):
   284	    nref = len(prob["ref_calls"])
   285	    out = []
   286	    for slot, call in M.judged_pairs(rec["calls"], nref):
   287	        if slot is None:
   288	            out.append((slot, None, None, None))
   289	            continue
   290	        ref = prob["ref_calls"][slot]
   291	        ref_m = M.ref_machine(ref)
   292	        head = call.get("program_head") or ""
   293	        text, how = M.program_text(head, rec.get("response_tail") or "", tok, call.get("call_tokens"))
   294	        judged = M.judge_call(tok, call, ref, ref_m, text, how)
   295	        out.append((slot, judged, ref, ref_m))
   296	    return out
   297	
   298	
   299	def sym_json(sym):
   300	    if isinstance(sym, tuple):
   301	        return list(sym)
   302	    return sym
   303	
   304	
   305	def word_json(word):
   306	    return [sym_json(s) for s in word]
   307	
   308	
   309	def separates(written, ref_m, word):
   310	    return run(written, word) != run(ref_m, word)
   311	
   312	
   313	def build_cases(group, problem, words, vocab, written, ref_m, question, answer_kind):
   314	    cases, skips = [], []
   315	    ordinal = problem["ordinal"]
   316	    which = ordinal % 3
   317	    para = paraphrase(question, which)
   318	    for i, word in enumerate(words):
   319	        rendered = render_word(word, vocab)
   320	        base = {
   321	            "id": f"{problem['pid']}-w{i}",
   322	            "group": group,
   323	            "pid": problem["pid"],
   324	            "tag": problem["tag"],
   325	            "index": problem["index"],
   326	            "template": problem["template"],
   327	            "cell": problem["cell"],
   328	            "slot": problem["slot"],
   329	            "word_index": i,
   330	            "word": word_json(word),
   331	            "answer_kind": answer_kind,
   332	            "para_template": which,
   333	            "smoke": False,
   334	        }
   335	        if rendered is None:
   336	            skips.append({**base, "why": "unrendered"})
   337	            continue
   338	        ref_state = moore_final(ref_m, word)
   339	        prog_state = moore_final(written, word)
   340	        ref_ans = project(problem["template"], question, ref_m, ref_state)
   341	        prog_ans = project(problem["template"], question, ref_m, prog_state)
   342	        if ref_ans is None:
   343	            skips.append({**base, "why": "ref_unreadable", "rendered": rendered})
   344	            continue
   345	        cases.append({
   346	            **base,
   347	            "rendered": rendered,
   348	            "ref_answer": ref_ans,
   349	            "prog_answer": prog_ans,
   350	            "separates_machine": separates(written, ref_m, word),
   351	            "separates_answer": prog_ans is not None and prog_ans != ref_ans,
   352	        })
   353	    return cases, skips, para
   354

    10	    "/Users/neo/.cache/huggingface/hub/models--Qwen--Qwen3-4B/snapshots/"

    14	_CONTRACTIONS = ("'s", "'t", "'re", "'ve", "'m", "'ll", "'d")

    17	def _bytes_to_unicode():
   124	def pretokenize(text):
   125	    out = []
   126	    i = 0
   127	    n = len(text)
   128	    while i < n:
   129	        if text[i] == "'":
   130	            low = text[i : i + 3].lower()
   131	            hit = None
   132	            for c in _CONTRACTIONS:
   133	                if low.startswith(c):
   134	                    hit = text[i : i + len(c)]
   135	                    break
   136	            if hit is not None:
   137	                out.append(hit)
   138	                i += len(hit)
   139	                continue
   140	        m = _match_letters(text, i)
   141	        if m:
   142	            out.append(m)
   143	            i += len(m)
   144	            continue
   145	        if _is_N(text[i]):
   146	            out.append(text[i])
   147	            i += 1
   148	            continue
   149	        m = _match_punct(text, i)
   150	        if m:
   151	            out.append(m)
   152	            i += len(m)
   153	            continue
   154	        m = _match_newline_run(text, i)
   155	        if m:
   156	            out.append(m)
   157	            i += len(m)
   158	            continue
   159	        m = _match_ws_not_before_word(text, i)
   160	        if m:
   161	            out.append(m)
   162	            i += len(m)
   163	            continue
   164	        m = _match_ws(text, i)
   165	        if m:
   166	            out.append(m)
   167	            i += len(m)
   168	            continue
   169	        raise ValueError(f"pretokeniser stuck at {text[i : i + 20]!r}")
   170	    return out

   183	class QwenTok:
   184	    def __init__(self, snap=SNAP):
   185	        snap = Path(snap)
   186	        raw = json.loads((snap / "tokenizer.json").read_text())
   187	        self.vocab = raw["model"]["vocab"]
   188	        self.id_to_tok = {i: t for t, i in self.vocab.items()}
   189	        ranks = {}
   190	        for i, line in enumerate((snap / "merges.txt").read_text().splitlines()):
   191	            if i == 0 and line.startswith("#"):
   192	                continue
   193	            a, b = line.split(" ", 1)
   194	            ranks[(a, b)] = len(ranks)
   195	        self.ranks = ranks
   196	        self.special = {}
   197	        self.special_id = {}
   198	        for tok in raw["added_tokens"]:
   199	            self.special[tok["content"]] = tok["id"]
   200	            self.special_id[tok["id"]] = tok["content"]
   201	        self._specials = sorted(self.special, key=len, reverse=True)
   202	        b2u = _bytes_to_unicode()
   203	        self.b2u = b2u
   204	        self.u2b = {c: b for b, c in b2u.items()}
   205	
   206	    def token_to_id(self, text):
   207	        if text in self.special:
   208	            return self.special[text]
   209	        ids = self.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False).ids
   210	        if len(ids) == 1:
   211	            return ids[0]
   212	        return None
   213	
   214	    def encode(self, text, add_special_tokens=False):
   215	        del add_special_tokens
   216	        # Offsets index `text` as passed in. Questions and programs in these records are already NFC;
   217	        # normalise only when it is a no-op so a walk over the original string stays aligned.
   218	        if unicodedata.normalize("NFC", text) != text:
   219	            text = unicodedata.normalize("NFC", text)
   220	        ids, offsets = [], []
   221	        for kind, piece, start in self._split_special(text):
   222	            if kind == "special":
   223	                ids.append(self.special[piece])
   224	                offsets.append((start, start + len(piece)))
   225	            else:
   226	                pos = start
   227	                for pre in pretokenize(piece):
   228	                    at = pos - start
   229	                    if not piece.startswith(pre, at):
   230	                        raise ValueError("pretokeniser left a gap")
   231	                    ids_pre = self._bpe(pre)
   232	                    spans = self._spans(pre, ids_pre)
   233	                    for i, (s, e) in zip(ids_pre, spans):
   234	                        ids.append(i)
   235	                        offsets.append((start + at + s, start + at + e))
   236	                    pos += len(pre)
   237	        return _Encoding(ids, offsets)
   238	
   239	    def decode(self, ids):
   240	        if isinstance(ids, int):
   241	            ids = [ids]
   242	        parts = []
   243	        buf = []
   244	        for i in ids:
   245	            if i in self.special_id:
   246	                if buf:
   247	                    parts.append(self._decode_buf(buf))
   248	                    buf = []
   249	                parts.append(self.special_id[i])
   250	            else:
   251	                buf.append(self.id_to_tok[i])
   252	        if buf:
   253	            parts.append(self._decode_buf(buf))
   254	        return "".join(parts)
   255	
   256	    def _decode_buf(self, pieces):

def dist(a,b):
 q=collections.deque([(a.start,b.start,())]); seen=set()
 while q:
  s,t,w=q.popleft()
  if (s,t) in seen:continue
  seen.add((s,t))

   nw=w+(x,)

    64	def make_problems(tok, cells, seed, mixed_digits=100):
    65	    """The same problems for every model: one seeded stream per cell, in cell order."""
    66	    probs = []
    67	    for ci, (t, split, lengths, n) in enumerate(cells):
    68	        for L in lengths:
    69	            rng = random.Random(f"{seed}|{t}|{split}|{L}")
    70	            for j in range(n):
    71	                kw = {"digits": mixed_digits} if t in D.MIXED_TEMPLATES else {}
    72	                ex = D.example(tok, rng, t, L, split, **kw)
    73	                probs.append({"cell": f"{t}|{split}|L{L}", "template": t, "split": split, "L": L,
    74	                              "question": ex["question"], "answer": ex["answer"], "answer_kind": ex["answer_kind"],
    75	                              "options": ex.get("options"), "ref_calls": ex["ref_calls"],
    76	                              "truth_values": ex["truth_values"], "phrasing": ex["phrasing"],
    77	                              "heldout_phrasing": ex.get("heldout_phrasing", False),
    78	                              "digits": ex.get("digits")})
    79	    return probs

   170	# ------------------------------------------------------------------ scoring a call against its reference

   172	def call_vs_ref(tok, prog, reading, ref):
   173	    """compile: the tables (tape) or the table's operation (numbers) equal the reference's; reading: the clock's tape
   174	    or numbers equal the reference's."""
   175	    if ref is None or prog is None or prog.get("error"):
   176	        return None, None
   177	    if ref["mode"] != prog["mode"]:
   178	        return False, False
   179	    if ref["mode"] == "tape":
   180	        want = [{"start": pb_lit.name_id(tok, t["start"]),
   181	                 "rows": {(pb_lit.name_id(tok, s), pb_lit.name_id(tok, e)): pb_lit.name_id(tok, x)
   182	                          for s, e, x in t["rows"]}} for t in ref["tables"]]
   183	        got = [{"start": r["start"], "rows": {(s, e): t for s, e, t in r["rows"]}} for r in prog["registers"]]
   184	        tape = [pb_lit.name_id(tok, e) for e in ref["tape"]]
   185	        return got == want, reading is not None and reading.get("tape") == tape
   186	    reg = prog["registers"][0]
   187	    table = {row[:3]: row[3:] for row in reg["rows"]}
   188	    d = [A.digit_id(tok, x) for x in range(10)]
   189	    want = {(d[int(r[0])], d[int(r[1])], d[int(r[2])]): (d[int(r[3])], d[int(r[4])]) for r in R.table_rows(ref["op"])}
   190	    comp = table == want and len(reg["rows"]) == 200 and reg["start"] == d[0]
   191	    return comp, reading is not None and reading.get("numbers") == list(ref["numbers"])

   196	def run_loop(llm, tok, probs, mode, executor, args, log=print, progress_path=None):
   197	    from vllm import SamplingParams, TokensPrompt
   198	    enc = lambda s: R.enc(tok, s)  # noqa: E731
   245	                s["done"], s["finish"] = True, "hard stop"
   246	                continue
   247	            s["resp"] += ids
   248	            n_exec += 1
   249	            if mode == "plain":
   250	                s["resp"] += opener
   251	                s["segments"].append({"type": "call", "ids": call, "plain": True})
   252	                s["calls"].append({"plain": True})
   253	                continue
   254	            prog = R.parse_call(tok, call) if call else {"mode": None, "error": "no program", "registers": []}
   255	            text = tok.decode(s["resp"])
   256	            reading = R.read_call(tok, p["question"], text, prog, s["results"]) if call else {
   257	                "error": "no program", "spans": [], "how": []}

   266	                run, info = executor(prog, reading, full)
   267	                fin = R.final_for(tok, prog, run)
   268	                value = R.result_value(tok, fin)
   269	                oracle = R.execute_oracle(prog, reading)
   270	                rec.update(info, full_real=full, run_exact=(run["finals"] == oracle["finals"] and
   271	                                                            run.get("outputs") == oracle.get("outputs")),
   272	                           certified=all(x["certified"] for x in info["registers"]),
   273	                           min_margin=min((x["min_margin"] for x in info["registers"]), default=None),
   274	                           events=len(reading.get("tape") or reading.get("pairs") or []))
   275	                if prog["mode"] == "numbers":
   276	                    rec["numbers_len"] = [len(x) for x in reading["numbers"]]
   277	            k_ok = sum(1 for c in s["calls"] if not c.get("error") and not c.get("plain"))
   278	            ref = p["ref_calls"][k_ok] if not rec["error"] and k_ok < len(p["ref_calls"]) else None
   279	            comp, readok = call_vs_ref(tok, prog, reading if not rec["error"] else None, ref)
   280	            rec.update(value=value, compile=comp, reading_exact=readok, call_tokens=len(call),
   281	                       program_text=tok.decode(call))
   282	            s["calls"].append(rec)
   283	            s["segments"].append({"type": "call", "ids": call, "call": len(s["calls"]) - 1})
   284	            s["segments"].append({"type": "final", "ids": fin})
   285	            s["results"].append(value)
   286	            s["resp"] += fin
   287	            if not rec["error"]:
    80	        for a in range(10):
    81	            for c in range(10):
    82	                v = a - c - b
    83	                rows.append((str(b), str(a), str(c), str(1 if v < 0 else 0), str(v % 10)))
    84	    return rows

    87	def table_rows(op):
    88	    return A.adder_rows() if op == "add" else sub_rows()
    89	
    90	
    91	def true_run(op, n1, n2):
    92	    """(outputs least significant first, final state) of the true table over the digit pairs."""
    93	    st, outs = 0, []
    94	    for a, b in A.digit_pairs(n1, n2):
    95	        if op == "add":
    96	            st, o = divmod(st + int(a) + int(b), 10)
    97	        else:
    98	            v = int(a) - int(b) - st
    99	            st, o = (1 if v < 0 else 0), v % 10
   100	        outs.append(str(o))
   101	    return outs, st
   102	
   103	
   104	def value_of_outputs(outs):
   105	    s = "".join(reversed(outs)).lstrip("0")
   106	    return s or "0"

   111	def _q(s):
   112	    return json.dumps(s, ensure_ascii=False)

   115	def number_line(which, ref):
   116	    """ref = ("quote", before, after) or ("result", k)."""
   117	    if ref[0] == "result":
   118	        return f"{which}: result {ref[1]}\n"
   119	    return f"{which}: after {_q(ref[1])} until {_q(ref[2])}\n"
   120	
   121	
   122	def number_program_ids(tok, op, first, second):
   123	    t = TABLES[op]
   124	    nl = A.nl_id(tok)
   125	    ids = enc(tok, f"<program>\ntask: {t['task']}\ninput: {t['input']}\n" + number_line("first", first)
   126	              + number_line("second", second) + f"register {t['reg']} (output {t['out']}): start 0\n")
   127	    for r in table_rows(op):
   128	        ids += [A.digit_id(tok, x) for x in r] + [nl]
   129	    return ids + enc(tok, f"answer: {t['answer']}\n</program>\n<exec/>")
   130	
   131	
   132	def number_program_text(op, first, second):
   133	    t = TABLES[op]
   134	    rows = "".join("".join(r) + "\n" for r in table_rows(op))
   135	    return (f"<program>\ntask: {t['task']}\ninput: {t['input']}\n" + number_line("first", first)
   136	            + number_line("second", second) + f"register {t['reg']} (output {t['out']}): start 0\n{rows}"
   137	            f"answer: {t['answer']}\n</program>\n<exec/>")
   138	
   139	
   140	def tape_program_ids(tok, lit):
   141	    """lit: {"task", "input", "anchors": (before, after), "registers": [{"name", "start", "rows": [(s, e, t)]}],
   142	    "answer_line"}; names are pb_lit names (one token, leading space where that is one token)."""
   143	    return pb_lit.program_ids(tok, lit)

   146	# ------------------------------------------------------------------ parsing a call (from its own ids)
   147	
   148	def last_call_start(tok, ids):
   149	    """Index of the token holding the '<' of the last '<program>' in ids (None if there is none)."""
   150	    for i in range(len(ids) - 1, -1, -1):
   151	        if "<" in tok.decode([ids[i]]) and "<program>" in tok.decode(ids[i:i + 6]):
   152	            return i
   153	    return None

   155	
   156	def parse_call(tok, ids):
   157	    """arith_core.parse_generated with result citations. numbers[which] = ("quote", b, a) | ("result", k) | None."""
   158	    nl = A.nl_id(tok)
   159	    regs, anchors, numbers, err, open_rows = [], None, {}, None, 0
   160	    for line in A.lines_of(tok, ids, nl):
   161	        text = tok.decode(line).strip("\n")
   162	        if text.startswith("tape:"):
   163	            m = A.TAPE_RE.match(text)
   164	            try:
   165	                anchors = (json.loads(m.group(1)), json.loads(m.group(2))) if m else None
   166	            except ValueError:
   167	                anchors = None
   168	        elif text.startswith(("first:", "second:")):
   169	            which = text.split(":", 1)[0]
   170	            m_res = RESULT_LINE_RE.match(text)
   171	            m_num = A.NUMBER_LINE_RE.match(text)
   172	            if m_res:
   173	                numbers[which] = ("result", int(m_res.group(2)))
   174	            else:
   175	                try:
   176	                    numbers[which] = ("quote", json.loads(m_num.group(2)), json.loads(m_num.group(3))) if m_num else None
   177	                except ValueError:
   178	                    numbers[which] = None
   179	        elif text.startswith("register "):
   180	            head = tok.decode(line[:-1])
   181	            m = A.MEALY_HEADER_RE.match(head)
   182	            if m:
   183	                regs.append({"name": m.group(1), "output": m.group(2), "kind": "mealy", "start": line[-1],
   184	                             "rows": [], "pi": []})
   185	                open_rows = A.MEALY_ROW
   186	                continue
   187	            m = A.MOORE_HEADER_RE.match(head)
   188	            if not m:
   189	                err = err or f"bad register line {text!r}"
   190	                continue
   191	            regs.append({"name": m.group(1), "output": None, "kind": "moore", "start": line[-1], "rows": [], "pi": []})
   192	            open_rows = 3
   193	            continue
   194	        if open_rows and len(line) == open_rows and all("\n" not in tok.decode([x]) for x in line):
   195	            regs[-1]["rows"].append(tuple(line))
   196	            regs[-1]["pi"] += list(line) + [nl]
   197	            continue
   198	        open_rows = 0
   199	    mode = "numbers" if numbers else "tape"
   200	    for r in regs:
   201	        states = {row[0] for row in r["rows"]}
   202	        keys = [row[:-1] if r["kind"] == "moore" else row[:3] for row in r["rows"]]
   203	        if r["kind"] == "moore":
   204	            size = len(states) * len({row[1] for row in r["rows"]})
   205	        else:
   206	            size = len(states) * len({row[1] for row in r["rows"]}) * len({row[2] for row in r["rows"]})
   207	        if len(keys) != len(set(keys)) or len(keys) != size:
   208	            err = err or f"register {r['name']}: table is not total and single-valued"
   209	        dest = 2 if r["kind"] == "moore" else 3
   210	        if any(row[dest] not in states for row in r["rows"]) or r["start"] not in states:
   211	            err = err or f"register {r['name']}: a destination or the start is not a state"
   212	    if not regs:
   213	        err = err or "no register"
   214	    if mode == "numbers":
   215	        if set(numbers) != {"first", "second"} or None in numbers.values():
   216	            err = err or "number mode needs a well-formed first: and second: line"
   217	        if len(regs) != 1 or regs[0]["kind"] != "mealy":
   218	            err = err or "number mode runs exactly one register with an output"
   219	    else:
   220	        if any(r["kind"] == "mealy" for r in regs):
   221	            err = err or "a register with an output needs first: and second: lines"
   222	        if anchors is None:
   223	            err = err or "no tape line"
   224	    return {"mode": mode, "registers": regs, "anchors": anchors, "numbers": numbers, "error": err}
   225	
    64	def wilson(k, n, z=1.959964):
    65	    if n == 0:
    66	        return [0.0, 1.0]
    67	    p = k / n
    68	    den = 1 + z * z / n
    69	    c = (p + z * z / (2 * n)) / den
    70	    h = z * math.sqrt(p * (1 - p) / n + z * z / (4 * n * n)) / den
    71	    return [0.0 if k == 0 else max(0.0, c - h), 1.0 if k == n else min(1.0, c + h)]
    72

    76	    if x <= 0:

   243	    total = args.steps
   244	    ckpt_steps = sorted({max(1, int(round(float(f) * total))) for f in args.ckpts.split(",")})
   245	
   270	        exs.sort(key=lambda e: len(e[0]))
   271	        micro, cur, cur_max = [], [], 0
   272	        for e in exs:
   273	            L = len(e[0])
   274	            if cur and max(cur_max, L) * (len(cur) + 1) > args.micro_tokens:
   275	                micro.append(cur)
   276	                cur, cur_max = [], 0
   277	            cur.append(e)
   278	            cur_max = max(cur_max, L)
   279	        if cur:
   280	            micro.append(cur)
   281	        n_target = max(1, sum(sum(1 for x in lab if x != -100) for _, lab, _ in exs))
   282	        for g in opt.param_groups:
   283	            g["lr"] = lr_at(step)
   284	        opt.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
   285	        loss_sum, correct, kl_sum, kl_n = 0.0, 0, 0.0, 0
   286	        pin_n = args.pinned_tables * args.pinned_blocks
   287	        pin_exs = sorted([pb_pin.pinned_example(pin_rng, pool, "train", args.pinned_blocks)
   288	                          for _ in range(args.pinned_tables)], key=lambda ex: len(ex["pi"]))
   289	        pin_loss, pin_ok = packed_loss(pb_pin.packed_batch, pin_exs, args.pinned_micro, max(1, pin_n))
   290	        me_n = args.mealy_tables * args.mealy_blocks * 2
   291	        me_exs = sorted([mealy_example(mealy_rng, tok, pool, "train", args.mealy_blocks, args.mealy_sub_frac)
   292	                         for _ in range(args.mealy_tables)], key=lambda ex: len(ex["pi"]))
   293	        for ex in me_exs:
   294	            mealy_kinds[ex["kind"]] = mealy_kinds.get(ex["kind"], 0) + 1
   295	        me_loss, me_ok = packed_loss(AD.mealy_packed_batch, me_exs, args.mealy_micro, max(1, me_n))
   296	        for mb in micro:
   297	            L = max(len(ids) for ids, _, _ in mb)
   298	            ids_t = torch.full((len(mb), L), stream.im_end, dtype=torch.long)
   299	            lab_t = torch.full((len(mb), L), -100, dtype=torch.long)
   300	            for i, (ids, lab, _) in enumerate(mb):
   301	                ids_t[i, :len(ids)] = torch.tensor(ids)
   302	                lab_t[i, :len(lab)] = torch.tensor(lab)
   303	                tokens_seen += len(ids)
   304	            ids_t, lab_t = ids_t.cuda(), lab_t.cuda()
   305	            hidden = core.model(input_ids=ids_t).last_hidden_state[:, :-1]
   306	            tgt = lab_t[:, 1:]
   307	            mask = tgt != -100
   308	            loss, n_ok = chunked_ce(core.lm_head, hidden[mask], tgt[mask])
   309	            total_loss = loss
   310	            kl_rows = torch.tensor([is_kl for _, _, is_kl in mb], device=ids_t.device)
   311	            if args.kl_coef and bool(kl_rows.any()):
   312	                with torch.no_grad(), model.disable_adapter():
   313	                    hb = core.model(input_ids=ids_t[kl_rows]).last_hidden_state[:, :-1]
   314	                km = mask[kl_rows]
   315	                kl = chunked_kl(core.lm_head, hidden[kl_rows][km], hb[km])
   316	                total_loss = total_loss + args.kl_coef * kl
   317	                kl_sum += kl.item()
   318	                kl_n += int(km.sum())
   319	                del hb, kl
   320	            (total_loss / n_target).backward()
   321	            loss_sum += loss.item()
   322	            correct += n_ok
   323	            del hidden, loss, total_loss
   324	        gn = torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(trainable, 1.0)
   325	        opt.step()
   326	        if step % 10 == 0 or step == total - 1:
   327	            rec = {"step": step, "loss": round(loss_sum / n_target, 5), "token_acc": round(correct / n_target, 5),
   328	                   "lr": lr_at(step), "grad_norm": round(float(gn), 4), "examples": len(exs),
   329	                   "elapsed": round(time.time() - t0, 1), "tokens_per_s": round(tokens_seen / (time.time() - t0), 1),
   330	                   "counts": dict(stream.counts), "pinned_loss": round(pin_loss / max(1, pin_n), 5),
   331	                   "pinned_acc": round(pin_ok / max(1, pin_n), 5), "mealy_loss": round(me_loss / max(1, me_n), 5),
   332	                   "mealy_acc": round(me_ok / max(1, me_n), 5), "mealy_kinds": dict(mealy_kinds)}
   333	            if kl_n:
   334	                rec.update(kl_per_token=round(kl_sum / kl_n, 5), kl_tokens=kl_n)
   335	            log.append(rec)
   336	            print(json.dumps(rec), flush=True)
   337	        if step + 1 in ckpt_steps:
   338	            path = out / f"adapter_step{step + 1}"
   339	            model.save_pretrained(path)
   340	            saved.append(str(path))
   341	            atomic_json(out / "train_record.json", {"args": vars(args), "trainable_params": n_train, "log": log,
   342	                                                     "saved": saved, "partial": step + 1 < total,
   343	                                                     "general_dropped_as_addition": dropped, "host": host_record(),
   344	                                                     "at": stamp()})

E4.md:9:1. **Many class-(a) suites cannot be asked.** The suite is built from the written machine. On the coin problems and the adder, that alphabet is not the question's event vocabulary (bigrams, bare digits, digit pairs), so those words are skipped. A perfect word-oracle still cannot catch a program whose distinguishing words are not phrases of the question. The catch rate on all 60 therefore sits well below E3's 57/60 exposure figure. The binding limit is the words that cannot be rendered, not the length of the suite.

codex
The 64-case bug changes the E3 headline: the written-built suite catches 58/60 class-(a) problems within 64 cases, not 60/60. The 16-case count remains 57/60. E4 also used a scoring rule changed after the smoke run; I’m recomputing both that rule and the frozen rule.

def parse(t,o,kind,frozen=False):

    80	    if not tape:
    81	        return None
    82	    for sep in (", ", " ", ","):
    83	        blob = sep.join(str(x) for x in tape)
    84	        i = question.find(blob)

    90	def rumor_gloss(question, tape):
    91	    matches = list(re.finditer(r"is lying|is honest", question))
    92	    if len(matches) != len(tape) + 1:
    93	        return None
    94	    phrase = {}
    95	    for m, sym in zip(matches[1:], tape):
    96	        expect = {"lies": "is lying", "truth": "is honest"}.get(sym)
    97	        if m.group(0) != expect:
    98	            return None
    99	        phrase[sym] = expect
   100	    if set(phrase) != set(tape):
   101	        return None
   102	    return {"kind": "gloss", "sep": ", ", "phrase": phrase}

   106	    if ref["mode"] != "tape":

   110	    if found:
   111	        return found
   112	    return rumor_gloss(question, tape)

   115	def render_word(word, vocab):
   116	    if vocab is None:
   117	        return None
   118	    if len(word) == 0:
   119	        return "none"
   120	    parts = []
   121	    for sym in word:
   122	        key = sym if isinstance(sym, str) else None
   123	        if key is None or key not in vocab["phrase"]:
   124	            return None
   125	        parts.append(vocab["phrase"][key])
   126	    return vocab["sep"].join(parts)

E4’s reported rates and intervals match the raw replies under its implemented scorer.     17	def _bytes_to_unicode():
    18	    bs = (
    19	        list(range(ord("!"), ord("~") + 1))
    20	        + list(range(ord("¡"), ord("¬") + 1))
    21	        + list(range(ord("®"), ord("ÿ") + 1))
    22	    )
    23	    cs = bs[:]
    24	    n = 0
    25	    for b in range(256):
    26	        if b not in bs:
    27	            bs.append(b)
    28	            cs.append(256 + n)
    29	            n += 1
    30	    return dict(zip(bs, [chr(c) for c in cs]))

    41	def _is_other(ch):
    42	    """Character class [^\\r\\n\\p{L}\\p{N}]."""
    43	    if ch in "\r\n":
    44	        return False
    45	    cat = unicodedata.category(ch)[0]

    57	def _match_letters(text, i):
    58	    n = len(text)
    59	    if i < n and _is_other(text[i]) and i + 1 < n and _is_L(text[i + 1]):
    60	        j = i + 2

    67	            j += 1

    73	    n = len(text)
    74	    j = i
    75	    if j < n and text[j] == " " and j + 1 < n and _is_punct(text[j + 1]):

    86	def _match_newline_run(text, i):
    87	    """\\s*[\\r\\n]+ : the longest whitespace prefix that ends on a newline."""
    88	    n = len(text)
    89	    j = i
    90	    last = None
    91	    while j < n and text[j].isspace():
    92	        if text[j] in "\r\n":
    93	            last = j
    94	        j += 1
    95	    if last is None:
    96	        return None
    97	    return text[i : last + 1]

   100	def _match_ws_not_before_word(text, i):
   101	    """\\s+(?!\\S), greedy with backtracking."""
   102	    n = len(text)
   103	    if i >= n or not text[i].isspace():
   104	        return None
   105	    j = i + 1
   106	    while j < n and text[j].isspace():
   107	        j += 1
   108	    if j == n:
   109	        return text[i:j]
   110	    if j - 1 > i:
   111	        return text[i : j - 1]
   112	    return None

   115	def _match_ws(text, i):
   116	    if i >= len(text) or not text[i].isspace():
   117	        return None
   118	    j = i + 1
   119	    while j < len(text) and text[j].isspace():
   120	        j += 1
   121	    return text[i:j]

   257	        raw = "".join(pieces)
   258	        return bytes(self.u2b[c] for c in raw).decode("utf-8")
   259	
   260	    def _spans(self, pre, ids):
   261	        """Character spans of each token inside `pre`."""
   262	        byte_pieces = []
   263	        for i in ids:
   264	            raw = "".join(self.id_to_tok[i])
   265	            byte_pieces.append(bytes(self.u2b[c] for c in raw))
   266	        encoded = pre.encode("utf-8")
   267	        if b"".join(byte_pieces) != encoded:
   268	            raise ValueError("token bytes do not cover the pretoken")
   269	        char_of_byte = []
   270	        for ci, ch in enumerate(pre):
   271	            char_of_byte.extend([ci] * len(ch.encode("utf-8")))
   272	        char_of_byte.append(len(pre))
   273	        spans = []
   274	        bpos = 0
   275	        for bp in byte_pieces:
   276	            spans.append((char_of_byte[bpos], char_of_byte[bpos + len(bp)]))
   277	            bpos += len(bp)
   278	        return spans
   279	
   280	    def _split_special(self, text):
   281	        out = []
   282	        i = 0
   283	        n = len(text)
   284	        while i < n:
   285	            hit = None
   286	            if text[i] == "<":
   287	                for s in self._specials:
   288	                    if text.startswith(s, i):
   289	                        hit = s
   290	                        break
   291	            if hit:
   292	                out.append(("special", hit, i))
   293	                i += len(hit)
   294	                continue
   295	            j = i + 1
   296	            cut = n
   297	            while j < n:
   298	                if text[j] == "<":
   299	                    for s in self._specials:
   300	                        if text.startswith(s, j):
   301	                            cut = j
   302	                            j = n
   303	                            break
   304	                j += 1
   305	            if cut != i:
   306	                out.append(("text", text[i:cut], i))
   307	            i = cut
   308	        return out

   310	    def _bpe(self, pre):
   311	        chars = "".join(self.b2u[b] for b in pre.encode("utf-8"))
   312	        word = tuple(chars)
   313	        pairs = _pairs(word)
   314	        if not pairs:
   315	            return [self.vocab[word[0]]] if word else []
   316	        while True:
   317	            bigram = min(pairs, key=lambda p: self.ranks.get(p, 10**12))
   318	            if bigram not in self.ranks:
   319	                break
   320	            first, second = bigram
   321	            merged = []
   322	            i = 0
   323	            while i < len(word):
   324	                if i < len(word) - 1 and word[i] == first and word[i + 1] == second:
   325	                    merged.append(first + second)
   326	                    i += 2
   327	                else:
   328	                    merged.append(word[i])
   329	                    i += 1
   330	            word = tuple(merged)
   331	            if len(word) == 1:
   332	                break
   333	            pairs = _pairs(word)
   334	        return [self.vocab[p] for p in word]
   335

   337	def load():
   338	    return QwenTok()
     1	"""
     2	Failure anatomy of the counted REASON runs (REASON.md §7): every wrong transcript in R1 and R2 (clock mode) gets one
     3	root cause, from its first failing call or, if every call ran, from where the answer went wrong; and separately whether
     4	it fell into the repair loop. Also: how often a call after an error line is identical to the failed one, and, for the
     5	frontier's parking result, how long a suffix of the log decides the answer.
     6	
     7	    python reason_anatomy.py
     8	"""
     9	
    10	from __future__ import annotations
    11	
    12	import collections
    13	import json
    14	import pathlib
    15	import random
    16	import re
    17	import sys
    18	
    19	HERE = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    20	RES = HERE / "results"
    21	sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
    22	
    23	TRUE_EVENTS = {"parking_fee": {"E", "X"}, "drone_compass": {"N", "S", "E", "W"}, "coin_add_or_sub": {"H", "T"},
    24	               "shelf_bill": None, "robot_charger": {"up", "down", "left", "right"}, "coin_bet": {"H", "T"},
    25	               "cups_count": None, "gate_heading": {"left", "right", "around"}, "parity_pick_sum": {"0", "1"}}
    26	NOTES_TEMPLATES = {"rumor_chain", "marble_trades", "truth_chain", "dance_swaps"}
    27	CLASSES = ("table (kind, alphabet or rows)", "quote or notes mismatch", "bare or malformed citation", "reading at length",
    28	           "words after exact calls", "no call", "other")
    29	
    30	
    31	def classify_error(template, err):
    32	    if err is None:
    33	        return None
    34	    if "number mode needs a well-formed" in err:
    35	        return "bare or malformed citation"
    36	    if "no result" in err:
    37	        return "bare or malformed citation"
    38	    if ("destination or the start is not a state" in err or "not total" in err or "bad register line" in err
    39	            or "register with an output" in err or "exactly one register" in err):
    40	        return "table (kind, alphabet or rows)"
    41	    m = re.match(r"not an event or separator: '(.*)'", err)
    42	    if m:
    43	        tokn = m.group(1)
    44	        ev = TRUE_EVENTS.get(template)
    45	        if template in NOTES_TEMPLATES:
    46	            return "quote or notes mismatch"
    47	        if ev and tokn in ev:
    48	            return "table (kind, alphabet or rows)"          # a true event the written table does not have
    49	        return "quote or notes mismatch"            # prose inside the quoted span
    50	    if "anchors not found" in err or "not a number" in err or "no tape line" in err or "crosses the span" in err:
    51	        return "quote or notes mismatch"
    52	    if "no program" in err:
    53	        return "other"
    54	    return "other"
    55	
    56	
    57	def anatomy(records, problems):
    58	    out = collections.defaultdict(lambda: collections.Counter())
    59	    loops = collections.defaultdict(lambda: [0, 0])
    60	    identical = [0, 0]
    61	    for r, p in zip(records, problems):
    62	        t = r["cell"].split("|")[0]
    63	        calls = r["calls"]
    64	        # identical resubmission after an error line
    65	        for a, b in zip(calls, calls[1:]):
    66	            if a.get("error") and a.get("error") != "too many calls" and b.get("program_head") is not None:
    67	                identical[1] += 1
    68	                identical[0] += a.get("program_head") == b.get("program_head")
    69	        if r["correct"]:
    70	            continue
    71	        loops[t][1] += 1
    72	        if r["finish"] == "hard stop" or sum(bool(c.get("error")) for c in calls) >= 3:
    73	            loops[t][0] += 1
    74	        first_err = next((c for c in calls if c.get("error") and c["error"] != "too many calls"), None)
    75	        if not calls:
    76	            cause = "no call" if r["finish"] == "stop" else "reading at length"
    77	        elif first_err is not None:
    78	            cause = classify_error(t, first_err["error"])
    79	        else:
    80	            ran = [c for c in calls if not c.get("error")]
    81	            if any(c.get("reading_exact") is False for c in ran) and t not in NOTES_TEMPLATES:
    82	                cause = "quote or notes mismatch"
    83	            elif any(c.get("compile") is False for c in ran) and t not in NOTES_TEMPLATES:
    84	                cause = "table (kind, alphabet or rows)"
    85	            elif r["finish"] in ("length", "context"):
    86	                cause = "reading at length"
    87	            else:
    88	                tv = p.get("truth_values") or []
    89	                vals = [c.get("value") for c in ran]
    90	                if len(vals) >= len(tv) and all(v is None or a == v for a, v in zip(vals, tv)):
    91	                    cause = "words after exact calls"
    92	                elif t in NOTES_TEMPLATES:
    93	                    cause = "reading at length" if p["L"] >= 1024 else "quote or notes mismatch"
    94	                else:
    95	                    cause = "other"
    96	        out[t][cause] += 1
    97	    return out, loops, identical
    98	
    99	
   100	def coupling_suffix(question):
   101	    """parking_fee: the shortest suffix of the log that decides the final count from every possible start."""
   102	    cap = int(re.search(r"(?:with|fits|garage:) (\d+) (?:spaces|spots|cars)", question).group(1))
   103	    log = re.findall(r"\b[EX]\b", question.split(":", 2)[-1] if "Gate log" in question else question)
   104	    for k in range(1, len(log) + 1):
   105	        ends = set()
   106	        for s in range(cap + 1):
   107	            v = s
   108	            for e in log[-k:]:
   109	                v = min(cap, v + 1) if e == "E" else max(0, v - 1)
   110	            ends.add(v)
    45	import arith_core as A  # noqa: E402
    46	import pb_lit  # noqa: E402
    47	import reason_core as R  # noqa: E402

    49	TRAIN_TEMPLATES = ("shelf_bill", "robot_charger", "coin_bet", "cups_count", "sales_compare", "deposit_chain",
    50	                   "truth_chain", "dance_swaps", "gate_heading", "parity_pick_sum")
    51	TEST_TEMPLATES = ("parking_fee", "four_total", "coin_add_or_sub", "drone_compass", "rumor_chain", "marble_trades")
    52	NUMBER_TEMPLATES = ("sales_compare", "deposit_chain", "four_total")
    53	SENTENCE_TEMPLATES = ("truth_chain", "dance_swaps", "rumor_chain", "marble_trades")
    54	MIXED_TEMPLATES = ("parity_pick_sum", "coin_add_or_sub")
    55	N_PHRASINGS, N_HELD = 6, 2

    58	def loguniform_int(rng, lo, hi):
    59	    return int(round(math.exp(rng.uniform(math.log(lo), math.log(hi)))))
    60	
    61	
    62	def random_number(rng, n):
    63	    if n == 1:
    64	        return str(rng.randint(0, 9))
    65	    return str(rng.randint(1, 9)) + "".join(rng.choice(A.DIGITS) for _ in range(n - 1))
    66	
    67	
    68	def pick_phrasing(rng, split, n=N_PHRASINGS, held=N_HELD, phrasing=None):
    69	    """train: one of the first n − held phrasings; test (held-out phrasing of a training template): one of the last."""
    70	    if phrasing is not None:
    71	        return phrasing
    72	    return rng.randrange(n - held) if split == "train" else n - held + rng.randrange(held)
    73	
    74	
    75	def _c(rng, xs):
    76	    return rng.choice(xs)
   967	def example(tok, rng, template, L, split="train", phrasing=None, tries=40, im_end=None, **kw):
   968	    """One verified composite (the reference transcript reproduces Python's truth through the reference clock)."""
   969	    fn = GENERATORS[template]
   970	    im_end = im_end if im_end is not None else tok.token_to_id("<|im_end|>")
   971	    last = None
   972	    for _ in range(tries):
   973	        ex = fn(tok, rng, L, split, phrasing=phrasing, **kw)
   974	        if ex is None:
   975	            continue
   976	        ex.update(template=template, split="test" if template in TEST_TEMPLATES else split,
   977	                  heldout_phrasing=template in TRAIN_TEMPLATES and ex["phrasing"] >= N_PHRASINGS - N_HELD)
   978	        try:
   979	            verify(tok, ex, im_end)
   980	        except (AssertionError, ValueError) as exc:

   530	def transcript_check(grouped, tok):
   531	    """Stitched text must match the saved transcript when both exist."""
   532	    by_q = {}
   533	    for split, rows in grouped.items():
   534	        for rec, prob, tag, i in rows:
   535	            by_q[(prob["cell"], prob["question"])] = (rec, prob)
   536	    mismatches = []
   537	    checked = 0
   538	    for split, job, tag in JOBS:
   539	        path = RES / job / f"{tag}_clock_transcripts.json"
   540	        data = json.loads(path.read_text())
   541	        for tr in data["transcripts"]:
   542	            hit = by_q.get((tr["cell"], tr["question"]))
   543	            if hit is None:
   544	                continue
   545	            rec, _prob = hit
   546	            calls = [s for s in tr["segments"] if s["type"] == "call" and "clock" in s]
   547	            if len(calls) != len(rec["calls"]):
   548	                mismatches.append((tr["cell"], "call-count", len(calls), len(rec["calls"])))
   549	                continue
   550	            for seg, call in zip(calls, rec["calls"]):
   551	                head = call.get("program_head") or ""
   552	                text = seg.get("text") or ""
   553	                if head and not text.startswith(head[: min(len(head), 80)]):
   554	                    mismatches.append((tr["cell"], "prefix", head[:40], text[:40]))
   555	                    continue
   556	                recovered, how = program_text(head, rec.get("response_tail") or "", tok, call.get("call_tokens"))
   557	                checked += 1
   558	                if how != "stitched":
   559	                    continue
   560	                end = text.find("</program>")
   561	                if end < 0:
   562	                    mismatches.append((tr["cell"], "no-close", how))
   563	                    continue
   564	                gold = text[: end + len("</program>")]
   565	                if recovered != gold:
   566	                    mismatches.append((tr["cell"], "stitch", len(recovered), len(gold)))
   567	    return checked, mismatches

    68	def pick_phrasing(rng, split, n=N_PHRASINGS, held=N_HELD, phrasing=None):
    69	    """train: one of the first n − held phrasings; test (held-out phrasing of a training template): one of the last."""

   377	def execute_oracle(prog, reading):
   378	    """Run the written tables in Python (the clock's run whenever the certificate passes). Returns
   379	    {"states": {name: [ids]}, "outputs": [ids] (number mode), "finals": {name: id}}."""
   380	    if prog["mode"] == "tape":
   381	        states, finals = {}, {}
   382	        for r in prog["registers"]:
   383	            delta = {(s, e): t for s, e, t in r["rows"]}
   384	            s, seq = r["start"], []
   385	            for e in reading["tape"]:
   386	                s = delta.get((s, e), s)
   387	                seq.append(s)
   388	            states[r["name"]], finals[r["name"]] = seq, s
   389	        return {"states": states, "finals": finals}
   390	    r = prog["registers"][0]
   391	    delta = {row[:3]: row[3:] for row in r["rows"]}
   392	    s, seq, outs = r["start"], [], []
   393	    for a, b in reading["pairs"]:
   394	        s, o = delta[(s, a, b)]
   395	        seq.append(s)
   396	        outs.append(o)
   397	    return {"states": {r["name"]: seq}, "outputs": outs, "finals": {r["name"]: s}}
   398

   401	    if prog["mode"] == "tape":
   402	        return tape_final_ids(tok, prog["registers"], run["finals"])
   403	    reg = prog["registers"][0]
   404	    return A.final_ids(tok, reg, run["finals"][reg["name"]], run["outputs"])

   407	def plain_run_ids(tok, prog, reading, run):
   408	    """The no-clock run text's ids (REASON_FORMAT §8), without the `\\n<run>\\n` opener and the closer."""
   409	    if prog["mode"] == "tape":
   410	        ids = []
   411	        for i, e in enumerate(reading["tape"]):
   412	            ids.append(e)
   413	            ids += [run["states"][r["name"]][i] for r in prog["registers"]]
   414	        return ids
   415	    reg = prog["registers"][0]
   416	    space = enc(tok, " ")
   417	    ids = []
   418	    for i, (a, b) in enumerate(reading["pairs"]):
   419	        if i:
   420	            ids += space
   421	        ids += [a, b, run["states"][reg["name"]][i], run["outputs"][i]]
   422	    return ids

The remaining count checks agree: E3’s classes, distance histogram, suite sizes, reference rebuilds, and transcript checks all reproduce. The main issues are the inflated ≤64 exposure count, certificate flags that omit required preconditions, and E4’s prompt and scoring choices. I’m separating those bugs from the claims the records still support.
codex
**E3’s “60/60 within 64 cases” is false: it is 58/60. E4’s published arithmetic reproduces, but it establishes failure of this prompting/scoring procedure—not that the model cannot be its own oracle.** No files were edited.

1. **Independent recomputation — BUG for E3 exposure; OK for E4’s implemented scoring.**

   I ran read-only Python scripts. For E3, I reconstructed machines from the original clock records using the shared tokenizer/parser, then independently implemented product search, partition refinement, access-word construction, suite enumeration, and aggregation. For E4, I independently parsed all 4,383 replies, grouped by problem, computed Boolean events, Pearson correlations, and Wilson intervals.

   **E3 class counts reproduce:**

   | Population | Wrong | (a) | (b) | (c) | (d) |
   |---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
   | R1 | 219 | 54 | 75 | 5 | 85 |
   | R2 | 45 | 6 | 32 | 1 | 6 |
   | Total | 264 | 60 | 107 | 6 | 91 |

   Thus class (a) is **24.66%, 13.33%, and 22.73%**, respectively. These match the report’s “54 of 219…6 of 45…60 of 264.” [E3.md:125–129](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/theory/research/theory/E3.md:125)

   **Suite exposure, all 60 class-(a) problems:**

   | Origin / bound | ≤16, recomputed | ≤64, reported → actual |
   |---|---:|---:|
   | Reference / reference states | 57 | **60 → 59** |
   | Reference / written states + 2 | 49 | **51 → 50** |
   | Written / reference states | 54 | 55 → 55 |
   | Written / written states + 2 | 57 | **60 → 58** |

   The offending code executes additional words and then sets **`exposed64 = True`** whenever *any* of them disagrees. Fix: leave the first-64 flag immutable; record later exposure separately. [machines.py:469–482](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/theory/research/theory/tests/e3/machines.py:469)

   Concrete false positives:

   - `r1a:108`: first written-suite disagreement is **case 85**, `W W W`.
   - `r2:20`: first written-suite disagreement is **case 345**, `down down down down up`; its reference/reference-bound witness is case **105**.

   Both saved records incorrectly say **`"exposed64": true`**. [e3_items.jsonl:109](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/theory/research/theory/tests/e3/e3_items.jsonl:109), [e3_items.jsonl:405](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/theory/research/theory/tests/e3/e3_items.jsonl:405)

   All reported suite cardinalities and size statistics reproduce. So do the distance histogram **{0:2, 1:51, 2:3, 3:2, 4:1, 5:1}**, template/anatomy tables, and correct-call counts **336 = 299 equivalent + 36 inequivalent + 1 unresolved**. The ten reference rebuilds and 476 transcript checks report zero mismatches when rerun.    | O3 | 9 | 0 | 36 | 35/517 | 83/944 |
   | O1∧O2 | 9 | — | 31 | — | — |
   | O1∨O3 | 16 | — | 59 | — | — |

   Every displayed proportion and Wilson endpoint matches to the reported precision. Correlations reproduce as **−0.058104, 0.134482, 0.587232**, each with n=27; the conditional means also match. [E4.md:83–111](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/theory/research/theory/E4.md:83)

   The item census also matches: **517/442 rendered/skipped class-(a) cases; 944/16 controls; 30 fully rendered problems; 27 rendered first separators; 16 unreadable program answers**. [E4.md:89](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/theory/research/theory/E4.md:89)

2. **Registered rules and mathematical validity — mixed verdicts.**

   - **OK: minimisation, equivalence, and observed class assignment.** Refinement starts from output classes and splits by successor classes; product search checks the union alphabet. My independent results agree. Classification implements the amendment: inequivalence first, unresolved next, then bad input, then good-but-wrong. The exact branch **`if any(x == "inequiv" for x in labels)`** implements class-(a) precedence. [machines.py:215–305](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/theory/research/theory/tests/e3/machines.py:215), [measure.py:440–453](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/theory/research/theory/tests/e3/measure.py:440), [E3.md:106–110](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/theory/research/theory/E3.md:106)

   - **BUG: “certifies” omits essential preconditions.** The code rejects only **`m_bound < n`**, then returns **`"certifies": True`**. It does not check the other machine’s state bound or matching alphabet. Five reference/reference-bound comparisons and eight written/written-plus-two comparisons exceed the bound on the other minimised machine; 27 pairs have different alphabets. Enforcing both prerequisites leaves **28, 25, 28, 25** eligible pairs in the four table rows above, rather than **60, 52, 55, 60**. Detection counts remain empirical observations. [machines.py:450–468](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/theory/research/theory/tests/e3/machines.py:450), [machines.py:495–507](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/theory/research/theory/tests/e3/machines.py:495)

     A wrong program **can pass the entire suite**: let the written machine loop on `a`, while the reference also accepts `b` and changes output on it. The written-built suite uses only **`spec_m.alphabet`**, never asks `b`, and returns agreement. I reproduced this with a two-state bound. Fix: establish a common external alphabet and enforce the implementation bound; distinguish suite construction from certification.

   - **BUG for general Moore final-output use; no numerical effect on these records.** The concatenation is correctly \(P\Sigma^{\le m-n}W\), but `characterise` starts with **`acc = []`**, rather than requiring ε. Furthermore, `run_from` returns the complete output trace: **`outs.append(m.out[s])`**. [machines.py:63–74](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/theory/research/theory/tests/e3/machines.py:63), [machines.py:332–361](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/theory/research/theory/tests/e3/machines.py:332), [machines.py:392–396](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/theory/research/theory/tests/e3/machines.py:392)

     Counterexample: unary chain \(0→1→2→2\), outputs \((0,0,1)\). Code chooses \(W=\{a\}\). Change only the initial output to 1: every tested nonempty word has the same **final** output, while ε differs. Fix: include ε and construct/score W under the intended final-output semantics. Here, Moore outputs are state identities, so ε is already selected; my final-output rescore changes none of the observed exposure counts. [machines.py:53–55](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/theory/research/theory/tests/e3/machines.py:53)

   - **BUG: E4 results do not use the frozen parser.** Registration says **“O1 and O2 are parsed on the text before `<program`”**; implementation removes completed program blocks and requires an `Answer:` span. The report acknowledges this was **“fixed after the smoke”**. [E4.md:47](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/theory/research/theory/E4.md:47), [score.py:109–146](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/theory/research/theory/tests/e4/score.py:109), [E4.md:319](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/theory/research/theory/E4.md:319)

     Applying the literal frozen extraction gives O1/O2 **0/60 catches**, **0 correct cases**, and false alarms **2/60 and 0/60**. Fix the reporting by separating registered and amended analyses; do not describe these as unchanged preregistered measurements.

   - **DESIGN FLAW: E4 cannot isolate oracle reasoning from interface failure.** All **1,461 O1 and 1,461 O2 replies contain programs** despite the instruction; cap hits are **780/597/1,154**, and unparsed replies **479/588/1,218**. These diagnostics reproduce. [E4.md:315](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/theory/research/theory/E4.md:315)

     Parsing demonstrably loses correct answers: O1 says **“Total: 45 dollars.”**, matching reference 45, but is unparsed without `Answer:`. O2 similarly says **“The answer is 8.”**, matching reference 8. Correcting just these two cases raises class-(a) accuracies to **79/517** and **56/517**, without changing their problem-level catch counts. [O1.jsonl:192](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/theory/research/theory/tests/e4/results/e4-full/O1.jsonl:192), [items.json:4721](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/theory/research/theory/tests/e4/items.json:4721), [O2.jsonl:180](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/theory/research/theory/tests/e4/results/e4-full/O2.jsonl:180), [items.json:4438](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/theory/research/theory/tests/e4/items.json:4438)

     A perfect oracle could catch only **26/60** under the existing renderer and answer projection. The correlation also selects a *machine* separator, which need not separate answers: one stored case says **`"separates_machine": true`**, yet both answers are **1**. [build_items.py:338–351](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/theory/research/theory/tests/e4/build_items.py:338), [items.json:6267–6270](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/theory/research/theory/tests/e4/items.json:6267)

3. **Leakage — OK for inspected evaluation paths; limited training assurance.**

   E4 stores reference/program answers, but constructs model input exclusively from the question/paraphrase and rendered word: **`"user": user_text(body, case["rendered"], sentence)`**. Answers and class labels are not inserted. E3’s reference access is intentional oracle-assisted evaluation. [e4_oracle.py:48–60](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/theory/research/theory/tests/e4/e4_oracle.py:48), [E3.md:29](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/theory/research/theory/E3.md:29)

   The inspected training generator explicitly calls **`RD.example(..., "train", ...)`**; held-out phrasings use a disjoint index range. This supports the generated-data split, but does not establish absence of overlap in the inherited adapter or auxiliary datasets. [reason_train.py:68–82](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/reason/research/reason/reason_train.py:68), [reason_data.py:68–72](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/reason/research/reason/reason_data.py:68), [train_record.json:10–29](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/reason/research/reason/results/reason-s1/train_record.json:10)

4. **Fair comparisons and checkpoint selection — OK for O1/O2; DESIGN FLAW for causal O1/O3 comparisons.**

   O1/O2 use the same freshly merged checkpoint, items, greedy decoding, and 512-token cap; paraphrasing is their intended difference. O3 changes adapter, thinking format, temperature, sampling, and cap to **4096** simultaneously. Consequently, its comparison cannot isolate training or oracle independence. [e4_oracle.py:129–172](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/theory/research/theory/tests/e4/e4_oracle.py:129)

   The adapter records **rank 64**, **500 steps**, **lr 0.0001**; O3 has no corresponding adaptation budget. Training applies pinned, Mealy, CE and applicable KL terms before saving checkpoints; E4 fixes `adapter_step500`, so no early-loss checkpoint-selection defect was found. [adapter_config.json:31](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/reason/research/reason/results/reason-s1/adapter_step500/adapter_config.json:31), [train_record.json:16–40](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/reason/research/reason/results/reason-s1/train_record.json:16), [reason_train.py:286–340](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/reason/research/reason/reason_train.py:286), [E4.md:51](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/theory/research/theory/E4.md:51)

5. **Figures/captions — OK: neither report contains plotted figures.** The relevant presentation defect is E3’s table caption: **“one of the shortest 16 or 64 words”** does not describe the buggy ≤64 calculation. [E3.md:211](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/theory/research/theory/E3.md:211)

**BUGs by severity:** High: inflated ≤64 exposure; unsound certification flags. Medium: frozen/parser mismatch; missing mandatory ε for general final-output Moore use.

**Headline claims that survive:**

- **E3:** the recorded class-(a) machines are caught by sufficiently many suite cases with the reference oracle; **57/60 within 16 and 58/60 within 64**, not universal 64-case coverage.
- **E3 scope:** class (a) is **60/264 failures**; this is not all wrong programs—**36 inequivalent calls occur in correct-answer problems**. [E3.md:230–231](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/theory/research/theory/E3.md:230)
- **E4:** this deployed prompt/parser procedure is unreliable; the broader **“no self-oracle”** conclusion does not survive the interface confounds.

**Minimal re-runs:** E3 needs only CPU rescoring and corrected certificate checks, no model generation. E4 first needs rescoring of existing replies under both documented rules, then a controlled inference rerun replacing the original event log with the suite word, explicitly specifying the answer format, and matching decoding budgets across compared arms. No retraining is needed.
